We introduce VākQA, a Telugu SQA benchmark of 2,001 factoid question-answer pairs across six domains, with 2.53 hours of speech audio, bilingual transcriptions, and human-verified reference answers. We first validate evaluation methods against human judgements: Gemini-as-a-judge best approximates human ratings but is non-uniformly strict, while open-weight judges systematically penalize correct Telugu answers that differ in surface form from the reference. Using this validated setup, we benchmark proprietary and open-weight models across input modality, language, and domain. We observe that Telugu phrasing retains cultural specificity that is lost in translation, speech input introduces phonetic confusions that alter question meaning, and cascaded ASR-MT errors compound progressively. VākQA is publicly released.

</details>


### [135] [D-Quant: Driftable Entropy Coding for KV Cache Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19880)
*Yi Su,Hong Liu,Guanghua Yu,Jianchen Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出D-Quant框架，通过引入漂移机制将熵编码的KV缓存表示转换为固定大小的比特流，实现并行注意力计算中的规则内存访问和高效解量化，解决传统固定宽度量化在低比特下信息损失严重的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存是部署大语言模型的主要瓶颈，现有量化方法多采用固定宽度量化，受限于量化级别数量随比特减少而指数下降，导致性能急剧下降；同时忽略KV值分布的非均匀性（如正态分布），无法有效利用高频与低频符号差异。

Method: 设计D-Quant框架，结合熵编码的优势对非均匀分布的KV值进行高效压缩，并引入‘漂移’机制将可变长度编码转为固定长度比特流，适配注意力计算中对规则内存布局的需求。

Result: 实验表明，D-Quant在保持高精度的同时显著降低KV缓存内存占用，支持更低比特量化且具备良好并行性，优于现有固定宽度量化方法。

Conclusion: D-Quant通过融合熵编码与漂移机制，在不牺牲计算效率的前提下实现了更优的KV缓存压缩效果，为大模型高效推理提供了新思路。

Abstract: The KV cache has become a major bottleneck in deploying LLMs, as its memory footprint grows linearly with sequence length and batch size, imposing substantial pressure on both memory capacity and bandwidth. Among various KV cache compression techniques, quantization is particularly attractive due to its effectiveness and ease of deployment. However, most existing methods rely on fixed-width quantization, where a $b$ bit representation is inherently limited to $2^b$ quantization levels. As the bit width decreases, the number of available levels shrinks exponentially, leading to severe information loss and rapid performance degradation. We further observe that fixed-width quantization fails to exploit the highly non-uniform distribution of KV cache. After rotation and normalization, KV values approximately follow a normal distribution, with most values concentrated near the center and only a small fraction appearing in the tails. Nevertheless, fixed-width coding allocates the same number of bits to frequent and rare symbols. Entropy coding naturally exploits such non-uniformity by assigning shorter codewords to frequent symbols and longer ones to rare symbols, substantially reducing the average number of bits required for representation. However, its variable-length output is not suited to highly parallel attention kernels, where efficient dequantization and computation rely on regular memory layouts and fixed-stride accesses. To bridge this gap, we propose \textbf{D-Quant}, a flexible KV cache quantization framework that introduces a \textbf{drift} mechanism to convert entropy-coded representations of each token into fixed-size bitstreams, enabling regular memory access and parallel dequantization within attention kernels.

</details>


### [136] [PetriBench: Benchmarking LLM Reasoning over Dynamic State Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19883)
*Pyrros Koussios,Benjamin Jäger,John Hua Yao,Ajay Sridhar,Violet Xiang,Chenhao Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PetriBench 是一个紧凑、自包含且可扩展的基准，用于评估大语言模型在动态状态空间中的推理能力，基于佩特里网（Petri nets）这一成熟的形式化方法。它将推理任务分为四类，涵盖不同范围和时间跨度，通过增加结构复杂度生成易、中、难三档题目，并以精确的真值进行评估。实验表明，各类模型的准确率随难度上升而下降，更难的任务揭示出不同的任务特定能力特征。此外，测试时计算资源提升性能，但对不同推理任务的影响各异；过程生成支持平滑的复杂度扩展。整体证明 PetriBench 提供了一个统一且可扩展的平台，用于探究 LLM 推理的优势、局限与缩放行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型推理评估基准存在任务单一、依赖外部知识或成本高昂等问题，缺乏对动态状态空间中推理能力的系统性评测手段。因此需要一种自包含、可扩展且能刻画多维度推理能力的新型基准。

Method: 采用佩特里网作为形式化建模工具，构建涵盖四种任务类型、三档难度级别的推理任务体系；通过结构化生成方式控制复杂度，使用精确真值进行评估；对多种开源与专有模型进行测试，并分析计算资源与任务类型对性能的影响。

Result: 模型准确率随任务难度递增而下降；高难度任务揭示出差异化的任务特定能力表现；测试时计算提升性能，但其效果因任务类型而异；过程生成支持平滑的复杂度扩展，验证了基准的可扩展性。

Conclusion: PetriBench 为评估大语言模型在动态状态空间中的推理能力提供了一个统一、自包含且可扩展的框架，能够有效揭示模型的推理优势、局限及缩放特性。

Abstract: Characterizing LLM reasoning remains an open challenge, as many existing benchmarks isolate specific reasoning skills, rely on external knowledge, or are costly to extend. We introduce PetriBench, a compact, fully self-contained, and scalable benchmark for evaluating LLM reasoning over dynamic state spaces using Petri nets, a mature formalism for modeling real-world concurrent and distributed systems. PetriBench organizes reasoning into four task families varying by scope and temporal horizon, with Easy, Medium, and Hard levels generated by increasing structural complexity and evaluated against exact ground truth. Across a diverse set of proprietary and open-weight models, accuracy decreases consistently with difficulty, while harder instances expose increasingly distinct task-specific capability profiles. Additional analyses show that test-time compute improves performance but interacts differently with different reasoning tasks, and that procedural generation yields smooth scaling with structural complexity. Together, these results show that PetriBench provides a unified and extensible setting for probing the strengths, limits, and scaling behavior of LLM reasoning.

</details>


### [137] [Evaluating Communicative Success in Machine-Translated Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19885)
*Faiz Ghifari Haznitrama,Alice Oh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可复用的三层次检查清单-评分框架，用于评估基于机器翻译的口译代理在对话中的表现，涵盖语义、语用和文化社交维度，以衡量自然性、意图传达和社交适当性。该框架适用于单轮和多轮交互场景，通过模拟用户回复并综合评估每轮对话的整体效果，相比传统翻译指标更能捕捉沟通失败。研究在12种语言方向上验证了10种口译系统，并发现从语义到文化社交层面的成功率逐级下降；传统指标忽略强模型的沟通缺陷。实验表明，情景上下文、结构化指令和文化背景对提升沟通成功率有帮助，但效果因系统而异。本研究为口译代理提供了新的评估基准与方法，强调沟通成功的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器翻译驱动的口译代理广泛用于跨语言实时对话，但现有评估标准仍基于孤立句子的忠实度，无法衡量实际沟通是否成功。真实对话中，语义准确之外还需考虑意图传递、自然表达及文化社交适宜性，这些方面被传统指标忽视。因此亟需一套能全面评估口译代理在对话中沟通成效的新方法。

Method: 提出一个三层次检查清单-评分框架，包含语义、语用和文化社交三个维度，支持单轮与多轮交互设置。在每轮对话中，模拟用户根据翻译内容回应，系统综合评分整个对话流的表现。通过受控扰动、跨评委对比与人工标注进行多角度验证。

Result: 结果显示，从语义到语用再到文化社交，沟通成功率呈现持续下降趋势；传统翻译指标未能识别出强模型在实际对话中的失败。情境上下文、结构化指令和文化信息显著提升沟通效果，但提升幅度因系统而异。该框架有效揭示了现有指标的局限性，并提供了一套可复用的评估方案。

Conclusion: 本研究构建了一个面向对话场景的口译代理评估框架与基准，证明了仅依赖翻译忠实度指标不足以反映真实沟通成效。未来应将通信成功作为核心评价目标，结合语义、语用与文化维度，推动口译技术向更自然、有效的人机协作演进。

Abstract: Interpreter agents built on machine translation (MT) increasingly mediate live conversation between people who do not share a language, yet we still evaluate them with metrics built for isolated sentences, which measure fidelity rather than whether communication succeeds. We introduce a reusable three-layer checklist-and-judge framework that evaluates interpreter-mediated conversation across semantic, pragmatic, and cultural-social dimensions, covering the naturalness, intent, and social appropriateness that fidelity metrics leave unmeasured. It runs in both single-turn and interactive multi-turn settings, where simulated users reply to translated messages as the conversation unfolds and each turn is scored alongside the conversation as a whole. We extensively validate it through controlled perturbations, cross-judge comparisons, and human annotations. Our main single-turn benchmark evaluates 10 interpreter setups across Arabic, Bengali, Indonesian, and Korean from 5,624 OpenSubtitles-derived scenarios spanning 12 translation directions, and our multi-turn study covers all 6 language pairs in scripted and live modes. Results show a consistent decline from semantic to pragmatic and cultural-social success, while conventional MT metrics overlook failures among stronger interpreters, and prompt ablations show that scenario context, structured instructions, and cultural context improve communicative success, although gains vary across setups. Our work thus provides an evaluation framework and benchmark for interpreter agents in conversation, and highlights the importance of communicative success alongside existing translation metrics.

</details>


### [138] [Generalization through Lexical Abstraction in Transformer Models: The Case of Functional Words](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19887)
*Giuseppe Samo,Vivi Nastase,Paola Merlo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨预训练变压器模型是否能像人类一样处理功能词（如代词、副词），并识别句子间语法和语义的平行性。通过分析词汇化与功能性句子在嵌入空间中的表示，发现功能词位于中心但具有独特性，而两类句子占据不同的子空间；混合训练可揭示共享结构，单独训练则无法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究预训练语言模型对功能词的理解能力，以及其能否捕捉句子间的语法和语义平行性，即类似'研究人员写了论文'与'他们写了它'之间的抽象对应关系。

Method: 将语言学问题映射到预训练变压器模型的嵌入空间中，比较名词与可替代它们的功能词在孤立及平行句子中的表示，并探查平行句子嵌入间的共享结构。

Result: 功能词在嵌入空间中位置居中且独特，符合其作为上下文通用占位符的行为；词汇化与功能性句子分布在不同子空间；仅使用单一类型数据训练无法揭示共享结构，而混合训练可使共享结构显现。

Conclusion: 预训练模型虽能编码功能词的中心性与泛化性，但需结合词汇化与功能性句子混合训练才能有效学习句子间的深层结构对应关系。

Abstract: Pronouns, adverbs and other functional words (such as they, her, somewhere, there) are often used in language to replace concrete nouns or phrases, when their properties - such as gender, grammatical number - provide sufficient information for the given context. Do pretrained transformer models encode such functional words in a manner that allows them to be used like humans do? Can language models recognize the syntactic and semantic parallelism of sentences such as "The researchers wrote the paper" and "They wrote it", which relies on such lexical abstraction?
  We map these linguistic questions into the embedding space of a pretrained transformer model, and compare representations of nouns, with the representations of the pronouns and adverbs that can replace these nouns, in isolation and in parallel lexicalized and functional sentences. We then probe for shared syntactic and semantic structure in the embeddings of parallel lexicalized and functional sentences.
  We find that functional words are located centrally compared to nouns, but are also distinct, which is congruent with their behaviour as place-holders in a wide variety of contexts.
  The analysis of the embeddings of parallel (lexicalized and functional) sentences show them inhabiting different subspaces of the embedding space. Experiments that distil the structural information of the sentence show that training on either type of data does not reveal the shared structure - because of the over-consistency of the vocabulary (in case of the functional data), and the too much variety (in case of the lexicalized versions). However, training with a mix of functional and lexicalized sentences, the shared structure emerges.

</details>


### [139] [KoNeoBench: A Curated Evaluation Dataset for LLM Understanding of Korean Neologisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19916)
*Soha Lee,Soojin Lee,Heesung Yang,Hyunju Song,Hyunji Lee,Jinsan An,Jeongwan Shin,Jin Hyun Park,Jun Lee,Hyeyoung Park,Kilim Nam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了KoNeoBench，一个用于评估大语言模型对韩语新词理解能力的基准测试。该基准基于2020年以来在线新闻中记录的1,785个韩语新词，经过专家词典学审查，每个条目包含用法示例、构词分析和词典式定义。研究设计了四个任务，并报告了近期模型及人类基线的表现。实验表明，当前大语言模型在恢复词源成分、区分语义类别和生成准确定义方面存在明显局限，揭示了当前模型在应对韩语近期词汇变化方面的挑战。KoNeoBench已公开于GitHub。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有韩语基准测试多聚焦于固定词汇，难以覆盖新兴词汇变化；且多数为英语导向设计，无法有效评估韩语中内容词与功能词素的组合特性。因此亟需一个专门针对韩语新词的评估基准，以更真实反映语言演变下的模型理解能力。

Method: 构建了KoNeoBench基准，收录1,785个2020年后出现的韩语新词，经专家词典学审查，提供使用例句、构词分析与词典定义。基于此资源，设计四类任务（如词源还原、语义分类、定义生成等），对多个主流大语言模型进行评估，并引入人类表现作为基线。

Result: 当前大语言模型在恢复新词构成成分、识别其语义类别以及生成准确定义方面表现不佳，尤其在处理韩语特有的词素组合结构时存在显著缺陷。人类表现显著优于模型，表明模型尚未充分掌握韩语新词的生成机制与语义演化规律。

Conclusion: KoNeoBench有效揭示了当前大语言模型在理解韩语新兴词汇方面的系统性不足，强调了语言动态性对模型评估的重要性。该基准为未来模型在跨语言、跨时间维度上的语义理解能力提升提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are typically evaluated on static benchmarks, even though natural language constantly evolves through newly emerging words and meanings. Existing Korean benchmarks are centered on established vocabulary and therefore provide limited coverage of such recent lexical change, and their English-oriented design makes it difficult to assess the typological properties of Korean, in which content words combine productively with functional morphemes. In this paper, we introduce KoNeoBench, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs' understanding of Korean neologisms. KoNeoBench is built on 1,785 Korean neologisms attested in online news since 2020 and curated through expert lexicographic review. Each entry provides usage examples, word-formation analyses, and dictionary-style definitions. Based on this resource, we define four tasks and report results on recent models, together with a human baseline. Our experiments show that current LLMs exhibit clear limitations in recovering source components, distinguishing semantic categories, and generating accurate definitions. These results reveal specific aspects of recent Korean lexical change that remain challenging for current LLMs. KoNeoBench is available at https://github.com/bcmilab/ko-neobench/ .

</details>


### [140] [Intrinsic Sequence-Likelihood Confidence in Retrieval-Dominated Extractive QA: Two Pre-Specified Negatives, and What They Do and Do Not Attribute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19942)
*Gunwoo Lee,Changmin Sung,Sang-Hwan Gwak,Ina Kim,Ji-Young Choi,Kyong-Ha Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在抽取式文档问答中，基于置信度的机制对性能提升有限，因为检索已能覆盖92-99.8%的最佳准确率。微调大语言模型于特定领域数据虽产生可信赖的置信度信号，但其用于查询筛选与答案信任的两种应用均未成功：四种模型家族下的蒸馏触发和路由-回避策略均未带来显著收益；序列似然置信度信号表现不佳（AUC 0.65-0.81），且无法通过校准改善。信心项在下游任务中无统计显著增益，仅在部分情况下影响模型是否通过评估标准。最终成果为一组预定义的负样本及其依赖关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在高检索覆盖率背景下，置信度驱动机制在抽取式文档问答中的有效性，尤其考察微调模型产生的置信度信号能否有效指导查询适应与答案选择。

Method: 在四个7-9B参数量的语言模型家族上，测试置信度驱动的蒸馏触发与路由-回避策略，使用预设标准评估其性能；分析序列似然作为置信度信号的表现，并进行消融实验验证信心项的影响。

Result: 检索已覆盖92-99.8%的最优准确率，置信度机制未带来实质性提升；置信度信号的AUC仅为0.65-0.81，且无法通过校准或温度调整改善；信心项在多数情况下无统计显著作用，仅在个别模型（如Gemma）中影响是否通过评估标准。

Conclusion: 在当前设定下，置信度信号无法有效支持模型决策，可用成果为明确预定义的负样本及其依赖关系。

Abstract: In extractive document question answering whose questions were generated from the passages that contain their answers -- so that retrieval recovers 92-99.8% of what any mode combination could reach, whatever its absolute accuracy -- confidence-driven mechanisms have little to gain. Fine-tuning an open language model on a specialized domain corpus yields a model whose own confidence is a tempting control signal: it could decide which queries warrant further adaptation, and which answers to trust. We evaluate both uses under criteria fixed before the runs were executed, across four 7-9B model families whose adaptation moved closed-book F1 by at most +0.03, and both fail: a distillation trigger on all four families, under its pre-specified three-step transfer budget, and a routing-and-abstention policy in its single-model pilot. Retrieval alone recovers 92-99.8% of best-case combined accuracy under every correctness criterion we test, leaving routers no meaningful gain. The sequence-likelihood signal is insufficient relative to that mode -- area under the receiver operating characteristic curve 0.65-0.81 under the registered criterion -- before adaptation as well as after, unchanged by scalar recalibration and not consistently improved by token-level temperature rescaling. And the finer diagnostics depend on the correctness criterion and on answer length; on the three adapted combinations where we could test it, selector ablations show no statistically detectable downstream benefit from the confidence term on any seed; on Gemma, removing it changes the selector from failing to passing both registered criteria. The usable product is a set of pre-specified negatives with their dependencies made explicit.

</details>


### [141] [Before the Arrest: Benchmarking LLMs on Criminal Profiling from Incomplete Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19965)
*Yutong Yao,Yanjie Cao,Guanhua Chen,Xu Yang,Junchao Wu,Zeyu Wu,Lidia S. Chao,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出PIJ数据集，包含来自五个国家的2500个真实谋杀案，旨在评估大语言模型（LLMs）在刑事调查全流程中的表现，涵盖犯罪侧写、犯罪过程重建和句子预测三个任务。结果显示，随着任务从显性事实提取转向隐性推理，模型性能显著下降，尤其在动机和受害者-嫌疑人关系等需要推断的任务上表现不佳。此外，模型与人类专家之间存在显著差距，并表现出性别、年龄和动机归因等方面的偏见。研究指出，基于不完整证据进行逮捕前推断仍是开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注逮捕后的法律任务，而对逮捕前基于不完整证据推断嫌疑人特征的关键问题缺乏探索。为填补这一空白，本文提出系统评估大语言模型在刑事调查全流程中的能力。

Method: 构建了包含2500个真实谋杀案的PIJ数据集，涵盖五个国家的真实案件；设计三个任务：犯罪侧写（需溯因推理）、犯罪过程重建（结构化信息抽取）和句子预测（法律演绎推理），用于评估9个强大大语言模型的表现。

Result: 模型性能随任务复杂度增加而系统性下降，尤其在需要推断性推理的任务中表现差；与人类专家相比存在显著差距，且在性别、年龄、动机归因等方面存在明显偏见。

Conclusion: 基于不完整证据进行逮捕前推断仍是一个未解决的重大挑战，当前大语言模型在涉及隐性推理的任务中表现有限，亟需改进。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to legal and criminal justice tasks, yet existing work focuses almost exclusively on post-arrest scenarios where the suspect's identity is already known, leaving the critical pre-arrest challenge of inferring suspect characteristics from incomplete evidence largely unexplored. To fill this gap, we introduce the Profiling, Investigation, and Judgment (PIJ), comprising 2,500 real homicide cases from five countries. PIJ evaluates LLMs across three tasks that span the entire criminal investigation pipeline: criminal profiling, which requires abductive reasoning to infer suspect attributes from fragmentary scene evidence, crime process reconstruction, which tests structured information extraction, and sentence prediction, which demands legal deductive reasoning. We evaluate 9 powerful LLMs and find that performance degrades systematically as tasks shift from explicit fact extraction to implicit reasoning over unknown suspect profiles. Categories requiring inferential reasoning, such as motivation and victim-offender relationships, remain the primary bottlenecks. Further analysis reveals substantial gaps between LLMs and human experts, along with pervasive biases in gender, age, and motive attribution. Our findings indicate that pre-arrest inference from incomplete evidence remains an open challenge.

</details>


### [142] [DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash: Pushing the Limits of KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19969)
*DeepSeek-AI,:,Anyi Xu,B. Li,Bangcai Lin,Bing Xue,BingCheng Xian,Bingzheng Xu,Bochao Wu,Bowei Zhang,Boyi Deng,C. C. Yu,Chao Jin,Chaofan Lin,Chen Dong,Chenbing Wang,Chenfan Feng,Chengda Lu,Chenggang Zhao,Chengqi Deng,Chengyuan Zhang,Chenhao Xu,Chenqi Zhao,Chenze Shao,Chuhao Wang,Chuqi Zhang,Damai Dai,Dejian Yang,Deli Chen,Di Huang,Di Wu,Donghao Li,Erhang Li,Eric Fu,F. Zhou,Fangwei Zhou,Fangyun Lin,Fangzhou Yuan,Feiyu Xia,Fucong Dai,Guangbo Hao,Guanglin Li,Guanting Chen,Guoai Cao,Guofan Fan,Guolai Meng,Guowei Li,Haichuan Zhang,Haiyang Ma,Haiyang Shen,Han Li,Han Yu,Han Zhang,Hangyuan Deng,Hanwei Xu,Hanxiang Xu,Hanxun Zhong,Hao Guo,Hao Jiang,Hao Li,Hao Qin,Haodong Wen,Haofen Liang,Haofeng Huang,Haohua Liu,Haoling Zhang,Haoming Luo,Haoran Yang,Haotian Xu,Haotian Yuan,Haoting Huang,Haowen Luo,Haoyang Cai,Haoyu Chen,Haozhe Ji,Hengran Zhang,Hengrui Wang,Hengxu Wu,Honghui Ding,Hongxuan Tang,Huadong Wang,Huanqi Cao,Huazuo Gao,Hui Qu,Hui Zeng,J. Yang,J. H. Jin,J. H. Zhang,J. X. Zou,Jia Yu,Jiahui Zhou,Jiajun Chen,Jialiang Huang,Jialin Zhao,Jiamin Tang,Jian Zhou,Jianan Tong,Jianwen Li,Jiaqi Zhu,Jiarui Wang,Jiasheng Ye,Jiashi Li,Jiaxin Xu,Jiaying Ding,Jibai Lu,Jiewen Hu,Jin Yan,Jincheng Zhai,Jingchang Chen,Jingcheng Hu,Jingli Zhou,Jingsheng Xu,Jingting Xiang,Jingyan Yun,Jingyang Yuan,Jingyuan Cheng,Jinhua Zhu,Jinpeng Wang,Jinyi Chen,Jinyi Hu,Jiping Yu,Jueliang Guo,Junbo Pei,Junbo Sun,Junguang Jiang,Junjie Qiu,Junkang Zhou,Junqi Liu,Junren Li,Junxian Li,Junxiao Song,Junyi Guo,Kai Dong,Kaifeng Chen,Kaige Gao,Kang Guan,Kangdong Yuan,Ke Hong,Ke Xu,Kefan Zhao,Kexin Ji,Kexin Zhang,Kexing Zhou,Kuai Yu,Lan Zhang,Lean Wang,Lecong Zhang,Lei Wang,Letian Gao,Liang Zhao,Liansheng Xu,Lihua Guo,Lingxiao Luo,Lingyue Fu,Litao Deng,Litong Wang,Liyue Zhang,Longhao Chen,Lu Chen,Luotian Huang,Luyao Ma,Luyao Wang,M. S. Di,Max Mei,Menghao Ye,Miao Cui,Mingchuan Zhang,Minghua Zhang,Minghui Tang,Mingjing Zhang,Mingqi Wei,Mingshu Chen,Mingxing Liu,Mingxu Zhou,Mingyu Xu,Mingyu Yang,Mingze Wang,Muyang Chen,Ni Shentu,Ning Wang,Niufang Ning,Panpan Huang,Peixin Cong,Peiyi Wang,Peiyuan Xin,Pengfei Ren,Pengfei Yan,Pengle Zhang,Qi Kang,Qi Tang,Qiancheng Wang,Qiang Li,Qihao Zhu,Qingyang Li,Qinyu Chen,Qiushi Du,Qizhou Guo,Rongxian Xu,Rui Ding,Rui Hu,Rui Tian,Rui Yu,Ruidong Zhu,Ruifan Xu,Ruihan Yang,Ruihang Xia,Ruijie Lu,Ruilin Geng,Ruipeng Hong,Ruiqi Ge,Ruisong Zhang,Ruize Sun,Ruizhe Pan,Runji Wang,Runqian Chen,Runxin Xu,Ruohong Tian,Ruomeng Shen,Ruoyu Zhang,Ryan X.,S. Zhang,Meng Li,Miao Lin,Miaojun Wang,Min Zhang,Mingming Li,Mingming Wang,Mingze Yin,Minmin Han,Nan Cao,Ning Wang,Ningxin Ma,Panpan Wang,Peihan Lin,Peng Sun,Peng Zhang,Qian Ying,Qiang Xiang,Qiao Wang,Qingmiao Mao,Qiwei Jiang,Rongli Jin,Ruyi Chen,Sha Tao,Shangmian Sun,Shaoqing Wu,Shichao Zou,Si Lei,Tianyang Zhang,Tianyu Sun,Tingting Yin,W. L. Xiao,Wei An,Wei Li,Wei Wang,Weiwei Lin,Wenqing Hou,X. Lin,Xiangfei Meng,Xianzhu Huang,Xiao Peng,Xiaoqian Li,Xiaoting Zhang,Xiaowen Sun,Xiaoxiang Wang,Xiaoyu Ye,Xinrou Zhang,Xinyu Zhang,Xue Cao,Xueyin Chen,Yanan Zhou,Yanhong Xu,Yao Xia,Yao Xu,Yi Shao,Yihong Zhang,Yiling Ma,Ying Tang,Yining Lou,Yiru Chen,Yishi Piao,Yixuan Chen,Yong Xiong,Yuchen Xuan,Yuehan Yang,Yuer Xu,Yukun Zha,Yunxian Ma,Yuping Lin,Yuting Yan,Yutong Xie,Yuwen Sheng,Yuxuan Zhu,Zekai Zhang,Zhe Ju,Zhenzhen Lin,Zheren Gao,Zheyang Sun,Zhigang Yan,Zhongyu Wu,Zi Wang,Zihua Qu,Ziling Yan,Ziyi Wan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash is a 552B-parameter multimodal MoE model with a CED architecture, enabling efficient prefill (8B parameters/token) and decode (16B parameters/token). It reduces KV cache footprint to 890 bytes/token via CSA2 and FP4 caching, and further cuts persistent KV cache by 8× using SWA Bounded Replay. Trained on 45T tokens, it outperforms prior models in agentic tasks while lowering deployment costs.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To address the high computational, storage, and bandwidth costs of long-horizon agents, especially due to expensive prefill and large KV caches in long-context models.

Method: Uses Causal Encoder-Decoder architecture, CSAs2 for cross-layer KV reuse, FP4 quantization for KV caching, and SWA Bounded Replay for persistent cache optimization.

Result: Achieves 1/4 reduction in global KV cache footprint and 1/8 reduction in persistent KV cache compared to DeepSeek-V4-Flash, with superior performance in agentic and multimodal tasks.

Conclusion: DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash significantly improves cost efficiency and scalability for long-context agentic workloads through architectural innovations and optimized caching, making large-scale deployment feasible.

Abstract: The widespread adoption of long-horizon agents has made model workloads increasingly input-heavy. Although prior work has substantially reduced the cost of long-context computation, prefill remains computationally expensive, and large KV caches continue to strain HBM and SSD capacity and data-transfer bandwidth. Together, these compute, storage, and bandwidth demands constitute the primary bottleneck to further lowering deployment costs. To address this challenge, we introduce DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash, a multimodal Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model with 552B backbone parameters and support for contexts of up to one million tokens. With its Causal Encoder-Decoder (CED) architecture, the model activates 16B parameters per token during decode but only 8B parameters during prefill, substantially improving cost efficiency for agentic workloads. To push the limits of KV cache compression, DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash combines cross-layer KV cache reuse in Compressed Sparse Attention 2 (CSA2) with FP4 KV caching. These designs reduce its global KV cache footprint (always in HBM) to 890 bytes per token, roughly 1/4 of the corresponding footprint of DeepSeek-V4-Flash. Further, through a dedicated deployment optimization known as SWA Bounded Replay, DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash reduces its persistent KV cache footprint (always on SSD or in host memory) to roughly 1/8 of that of DeepSeek-V4-Flash. Despite its much smaller KV cache footprint, the model delivers substantially better performance than the baseline. In addition, we streamline the DeepSeek-V4 architecture and introduce several efficient architectural extensions. We pretrain DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash on a multimodal corpus comprising 45T tokens and conduct comprehensive post-training, yielding strong performance across diverse text-based and multimodal agentic scenarios. Model checkpoints are available at https://huggingface.co/deepseek-ai/DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash.

</details>


### [143] [Benchmarking LLM Compliance with China AI Generated Content Regulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19989)
*Chenrui Cui,Hongye Fang,Lisha Song,Weichao Chen,Yue Zhu,Gang Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究针对大语言模型（LLM）在中文内容生成中的合规风险，基于中国现行AI生成内容监管要求，评估了20个主流大模型的合规表现。研究设计了一套包含2303个问题的新型评估框架，涵盖六个维度，其中包含203个自建宪法相关问题。通过多位评审员基于层级对齐记忆独立判断，发现国际模型在使用标准中文问题时仍表现出高合规性，主要差异体现在与意识形态对齐密切相关的维度。研究建立了符合中国法律要求的监管基准，为全球AI社区提供统一评估标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型的广泛应用，内容合规风险日益突出。现有研究多集中于英文语境，忽视了中文内容的复杂性。中国对AI生成内容有明确的监管要求，亟需建立符合本土法规的评估体系，以确保模型输出符合国家政策与价值观。

Method: 设计并实施一个包含2303个问题的评估框架，覆盖六类内容维度，包括203个自建宪法相关问题。采用多位评审员基于层级对齐记忆独立进行合规性判断，通过多轮共识达成最终结论。

Result: 国际主流大模型在标准中文问题下仍表现出较高的合规率，但不同模型在与意识形态紧密相关的维度上存在显著差异。研究成功构建了一个符合中国法律要求的统一监管基准，可用于评估中英文模型的合规表现。

Conclusion: 本研究不仅揭示了当前大模型在中文合规方面的现状，还为全球人工智能社区提供了可操作、合法合规的评估工具和基准，有助于推动负责任的AI发展。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of LLMs has led to escalating content compliance risks. Prior works have contributed to addressing these risks in the English context, downplaying the complexity of Chinese language content. This paper follows China's current AI-Generated content compliance requirements and provides evaluation results on 20 notable LLMs, offering insight into China's regulatory landscape. We design a novel framework to assess the compliance and refusal rates with 2303 questions spanning six distinct dimensions, including 203 self-constructed constitutional questions. The framework employs several judges to generate verdicts independently based on their hierarchical alignment memory. Our findings show that international models also exhibit high levels of compliance despite the use of standard Chinese questions, and the main differences may stem from dimensions closely related to ideological alignment. We establish a regulatory benchmark that enables the global AI community to evaluate both Chinese and non-Chinese LLMs under a unified set of legally grounded compliance requirements.

</details>


### [144] [Reading Emotions in the Token Space: Discriminative Adaptation of SpeechLLMs for Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20081)
*Hasindri Watawana,Sergio Burdisso,Esaú Villatoro-Tello,Manjunath K E,Kadri Hacioglu,Petr Motlicek,Andreas Stolcke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种判别式适配方法，通过分类头从语音大模型的隐藏状态读取最终提示词，实现单次前向传播的准确情绪分类，提升IEMOCAP数据集上的宏平均F1分数并消除生成式解码中的幻觉现象，尤其在真实语音识别转录文本上效果显著。该方法保持轻量级线性分类头，增强可解释性，揭示情绪方向与网络规模文本中的隐含偏见相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音大模型依赖生成式解码器进行情绪识别，存在输出标签超出目标集合、偏好高频类别的问题，缺乏判别性分类能力。需要一种无需修改骨干网络、能直接利用隐藏状态进行精准分类的方法，以实现更可靠的情绪识别并支持可解释性分析。

Method: 设计一个单一线性分类头，从语音大模型的最终提示词隐藏状态中提取特征，完成情绪分类；整个过程仅需一次前向传播，不改变原模型结构，实现生成式与判别式推理的公平对比。

Result: 在IEMOCAP数据集上，两种语音大模型架构均表现出更高的宏观F1分数，显著减少幻觉现象，尤其在真实语音识别转录文本上提升明显；分类头揭示的情绪方向映射出与网络文本中固有偏见相似的间接关联。

Conclusion: 所提出的判别式适配方法有效提升了语音大模型在情绪识别任务中的准确性与可靠性，同时增强了模型决策的可解释性，并揭示了模型学习到的情绪表示与训练数据中的社会偏见之间的联系。

Abstract: SpeechLLMs have shown strong potential for emotion recognition, yet they read the predicted emotion off a generative decoder not suited for classification: it can emit labels outside the target set and favors frequent classes. We propose a discriminative adaptation that reads the final prompt token's hidden state through a classification head, producing a label in one forward pass without modifying the backbone. Because this readout starts from the hidden state the model would otherwise decode, it gives a controlled comparison of generative and discriminative inference in an otherwise identical speechLLM. We keep the head a single linear layer, trading little accuracy for interpretability: each emotion becomes one direction in the LLM output token space, revealing associated tokens. On IEMOCAP, across two speechLLM architectures, it improves Macro F1 and removes hallucinations, with largest gains on realistic ASR transcripts. Our analysis reveals that these emotion directions encode indirect associations mirroring biases in web-scale text.

</details>


### [145] [Design of the IBM Granite 5.0 TurboCTC ASR Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20104)
*Brian Kingsbury,George Saon,Masayuki Suzuki,Hong-Kwang J. Kuo,Takashi Fukuda,Samuel Thomas,Vishal Sunder,Avihu Dekel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Granite 5.0 Turbo CTC 是一个4.7亿参数的编码器模型，采用金字塔式时间下采样、块对角自注意力和中间预测条件化等设计，在保持高准确率的同时显著提升推理速度。训练使用公开数据、新提出的Muon优化器和平衡数据采样；推理优化包括用线性层替代1×1卷积并优化注意力计算。该模型在英文短语音识别任务中达到Open ASR排行榜的最优性能-速度权衡，比最快竞品快一倍，且可免费使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在保证高精度的前提下，提升语音识别模型的推理速度，满足实际应用中对低延迟的需求。同时，希望使用公开数据和开源许可，推动开放语音识别技术的发展。

Method: 采用金字塔式时间下采样（通过步幅深度卷积实现）与Conformer块结合，使用块对角（分块）自注意力机制，引入中间层预测作为条件输入。训练阶段使用公开数据集，创新性地采用Muon优化器，并实施平衡数据采样策略。推理阶段通过将1×1卷积替换为线性层，以及优化注意力计算流程来加速。

Result: 模型在英文短语音识别任务中达到当前Open ASR排行榜的最佳速度-精度权衡表现，推理速度是最快竞争对手的两倍，同时保持了高准确率。模型支持宽松许可协议，可在Hugging Face平台下载使用。

Conclusion: Granite 5.0 Turbo CTC 成功实现了高效与高精度的平衡，是适用于实时语音识别场景的高性能轻量级模型，具有良好的实用性和可访问性。

Abstract: We describe the architecture, training methodology and inference speedups of Granite 5.0 Turbo CTC, a 470 million parameter encoder-only model with an excellent speed-accuracy tradeoff. The architecture uses pyramidal temporal subsampling within Conformer blocks using strided depthwise convolutions, block-diagonal (chunk-wise) self-attention, and conditioning on intermediate predictions from the middle layer. Training highlights are the use of only publicly available data, the novel use of a Muon optimizer, and balanced data sampling. Inference speedups include replacing 1 x 1 convolutions with linear layers and optimizing the attention computation in the Conformer blocks. Collectively, these result in a model that is on the speed-accuracy Pareto frontier of the Open ASR leaderboard for English short-form ASR while being twice as fast as the fastest competitor. The model can be used under a permissive license and downloaded from https://huggingface.co/ibm-granite/granite-speech-5.0-470m-turboctc.

</details>


### [146] [Fine-Tuning Models for Biomedical Relation Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20169)
*Claudiu Creanga,Liviu P. Dinu,Daniela Gifu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出预训练模型（PTMs）用于自动提取生物医学文本中的关系，聚焦于变异-表型领域。在SNPPhenA数据集上的评估表明，微调小型BERT模型（尤其是DeBERTa）表现优异，接近当前最先进水平。此外，精心微调Google的Gemini Pro 1.0在句子级和摘要级任务中均优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从海量生物医学文献中自动提取遗传变异与表型之间的关系，以应对大规模研究中信息提取的挑战，传统手动方式已无法满足需求。

Method: 采用预训练模型（如BERT、DeBERTa和Gemini Pro 1.0），通过微调策略在SNPPhenA数据集上进行变异-表型关系抽取，分别在句子级和摘要级任务中进行评估。

Result: 微调后的DeBERTa模型表现接近当前最先进水平；精心微调的Gemini Pro 1.0在句子级和摘要级任务中均超越现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 预训练模型，特别是经过精细微调的Gemini Pro 1.0，在生物医学文本中变异-表型关系抽取任务上展现出卓越性能，为自动化知识提取提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Next-Generation Sequencing has revolutionized the study of genetic mutations, enabling large-scale investigations into their roles in disease development. However, extracting meaningful insights from the vast amount of biomedical literature remains a complex challenge that cannot be addressed manually. In this paper, we present pre-trained models (PTMs) for the automatic extraction of relations from biomedical text, specifically targeting the variant-phenotype domain. Our evaluation on the SNPPhenA corpus demonstrates that fine-tuning small BERT-based models, particularly DeBERTa, yields strong performance, approaching the current state-of-the-art (SOTA). Additionally, our results indicate that carefully fine-tuning Google's Gemini Pro 1.0 outperforms the existing SOTA for both sentence-level tasks (where the model processes only the target sentence) and abstract-level tasks (where the model processes the entire abstract).

</details>


### [147] [To Copy or Not to Copy: Controlling Speculative Decoding via Intrinsic Model Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20186)
*Roy Eisenstadt,Ido Cohen,Edo Cohen-Karlik,Lior Wolf,Itamar Zimerman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SwitchSD提出一种自适应框架，通过轻量级探针识别大语言模型内部表示中的真实复制意图，实现神经草稿与基于上下文复制的动态切换，显著提升推理吞吐量（最高达15%），解决现有复制方法因误触发导致的性能下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码方法在神经草稿和基于上下文的复制之间存在根本权衡；复制方法虽在复制密集场景中速度快，但易因表面n-gram重叠产生误触发，导致吞吐量下降。

Method: 引入SwitchSD框架，利用轻量级探针分析目标模型内部表示，以高精度（AUC > 0.99）识别真实的复制意图，并据此动态选择使用神经草稿或上下文复制策略。

Result: 在Llama和Qwen系列模型上，SwitchSD相比EAGLE3等先进基线，实现最高15%的吞吐量提升，将复制从噪声启发式转变为可解释、模型感知的解码机制。

Conclusion: SwitchSD通过将复制视为模型的潜在控制信号，实现了更高效、更可靠的推测解码，为大语言模型推理加速提供了新范式。

Abstract: Speculative Decoding (SD) has significantly accelerated Large Language Model (LLM) inference, yet existing approaches face a fundamental tradeoff between two drafting strategies: neural drafting and context-based copying. Neural drafts (e.g., EAGLE3) provide robust performance across diverse text settings, while copy-based methods achieve higher speedups in copy-intensive regimes by generating candidates faster and exploiting long repetition spans for near-perfect speculation. We analyze existing copy-based methods and find that they are prone to accidental repetitions where surface-level n-gram overlap does not reflect a structural intent to copy, leading to false-positive triggers that ultimately degrade throughput. We introduce SwitchSD, an adaptive framework that treats copying as a latent control signal of the LLM. By training lightweight probes on the target model's internal representations, SwitchSD identifies genuine copy-intent with high precision (AUC > 0.99). This allows the system to dynamically switch between neural drafting (e.g., EAGLE) and context-based copying. Our results across Llama and Qwen families demonstrate throughput gains of up to 15% over state-of-the-art baselines like EAGLE3, effectively turning copying from a noisy heuristic into a principled, model-aware decoding regime.

</details>


### [148] [Foundations of Stochastic Lexical Calculus: Semantic Descent and Random Dynamics on Probability Simplices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20207)
*Matthew F Dixon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种可观测框架，用于判断语言模型生成的概率是否支持可序列化状态表示。理论部分定义了上下文语言的类型可测变换，构建最小封闭表示，并给出语义更新存在的充要条件；同时分析了不可约非封闭性和累积误差，证明在平均收缩条件下外部随机递归在概率单纯形上存在唯一且稳定。实验部分通过冻结实验验证可观测推论：原始提示条件概率不满足预设不变性门限；经提示特定校准后，一个通用三状态表示通过稳定性检验，并覆盖28/30未触及的八步路径（0.933，名义水平0.90）。结论表明，语言概率仅在验证封闭性、稳定性和覆盖性后，才可在声明的操作域内支持随机状态表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学系统需要基于证据更新的有意义状态不确定性，而大语言模型输出的是提示依赖的概率，二者不匹配。因此需建立语言概率与可更新状态表示之间的桥梁。

Method: 提出可观测框架，通过类型可测变换构造最小封闭表示，推导语义更新的充要条件；结合数学分析证明在平均收缩下外部随机递归的存在性、唯一性与稳定性；实证部分采用冻结实验测试校准前后语言概率的表现。

Result: 原始提示概率不满足不变性要求；校准后的三状态表示通过稳定性检验，覆盖28/30八步路径（0.933），高于0.90阈值。

Conclusion: 语言模型的概率仅在验证封闭性、稳定性和覆盖性后，才能在声明操作域内支持有效的随机状态表示。

Abstract: Large language models produce prompt-dependent probabilities over words, whereas scientific systems require uncertainty over meaningful states that can be updated as evidence arrives. We develop an observable framework for determining when language-derived probabilities support such a sequential state representation. Theoretically, we define typed measurable transformations of contextual language, construct a minimal closed representation, and give necessary and sufficient conditions for semantic updates to exist uniquely. We bound irreducible nonclosure and accumulated error, and under average contraction prove existence, uniqueness and stability of an external random recursion on a probability simplex. These results define a stochastic lexical calculus without attributing an internal calculus to the language model. Empirically, frozen experiments test the observable implications. Raw prompt-conditioned probabilities fail the prespecified invariance gate; after prompt-specific calibration, a common three-state representation passes the stability gates and covers 28 of 30 untouched eight-step paths, or 0.933 at nominal level 0.90. Accordingly, language probabilities support a stochastic state only conditionally on verified closure, stability and coverage within a declared operating domain.

</details>


### [149] [Before the Warning Comes Too Late: Incremental Phone-Scam Detection from Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20223)
*Khang Nhat Hoang Vo,Anh Trac Duc Dinh,Tai Tien Ta,Tho Quan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: StreamFraudNet 是一种用于弱监督增量电信诈骗检测的模型，通过处理原始语音音频中的重叠上下文窗口，在仅提供通话级别标签的情况下实现实时预测。该模型在英语基准测试中达到 0.9953 的 ROC-AUC，显著优于基线方法，并在低延迟下（首次预测仅需 10 秒，每 2 秒更新一次）运行速度快于实时。消融实验表明，循环时间建模是性能提升的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电信诈骗检测方法通常依赖完整通话记录或转录文本，难以实现低延迟、增量式预测；而真实场景中需在通话过程中尽早识别风险，因此需要一种无需转录、无时间标注、能从原始语音流中实时评估欺诈风险的模型。

Method: StreamFraudNet 使用冻结的自监督语音编码器对输入音频进行特征提取，采用重叠的有限上下文窗口处理，结合循环神经网络建模时间动态，并通过可学习聚合机制融合各窗口的隐状态得分，实现增量式预测。

Result: 在控制的英文基准上，StreamFraudNet 达到 0.9953 的 ROC-AUC，优于声学特征和平均池化基线，与强全局时间模型相当；首次预测仅需 10 秒，每 2 秒更新一次，且在实际服务器硬件上运行速度超过实时。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了仅基于原始语音即可实现高精度、低延迟的增量式欺诈风险评分，无需转录或时间标注；同时指出未来需引入延迟感知训练以进一步提升早期预测能力。

Abstract: We study weakly supervised incremental telecom fraud detection from raw telephone audio, where training provides only conversation-level labels and predictions must be updated before a call ends. We introduce StreamFraudNet, which processes incoming audio through overlapping bounded-context windows using a frozen self-supervised speech encoder, recurrent temporal modeling, and learned aggregation of latent window scores. On a controlled English benchmark, StreamFraudNet achieves a ROC--AUC of \(0.9953\), significantly outperforming acoustic and mean-pooling baselines while remaining competitive with strong global temporal models. The model produces its first prediction after 10 seconds of audio, updates every 2 seconds, and operates faster than real time on the evaluated server hardware. Ablations identify recurrent temporal context as the principal contributor to performance. These results demonstrate that fraud risk can be scored incrementally from raw speech without transcripts or temporal annotations, while highlighting the need for latency-aware training to improve early prediction.

</details>


### [150] [The Public Discourse Corpus (PDC): A Speaker-Attributed Dataset for Valence and Epistemic Modality with Target Speaker Participation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20232)
*Bo Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了首个针对公众人物访谈话语进行情感极性和认识模态联合标注的语料库——公共话语语料库（PDC）。该语料库包含来自100位发言人的998个视频，涵盖七个专业领域，经分割与筛选后共获得186,642句（310万词）。为确保保留视频中目标发言人的可分析话语，提出了五类标注体系‘目标发言人参与度（TSP）’，并展示了其标注一致性（κ=0.616）。采用基于音频的说话人分离流程（结合Whisper ASR与pyannote），实现了对目标发言人的精确提取。所有数据、工具与处理流程均已开源发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语料库缺乏对公众人物访谈中情感极性与认识模态的联合标注，且缺乏对目标发言人话语的精准识别方法。本研究旨在填补这一空白，提升对公共话语中情感与认知特征的分析能力。

Method: 构建了包含998个视频的公共话语语料库；提出目标发言人参与度（TSP）五级标注体系以识别目标发言内容；采用音频优先的说话人分离流程，结合Whisper ASR与pyannote实现高精度语音分割；所有标注工具、验证样本与处理流程均开源。

Result: 成功构建了首个公开可用的、经过情感极性与认识模态双重标注的公共话语语料库（PDC），包含186,642句、310万词；TSP标注具有良好的一致性（κ=0.616）；语音分离流程有效区分目标发言人与采访者/第三方发言；全部资源已通过Hugging Face平台开放共享。

Conclusion: 本研究不仅提供了一个高质量、多维度标注的公共话语语料库，还提出了一套可复用的方法论框架（TSP与音频优先的说话人分离），推动了公共话语分析领域的数据标准化与技术可复现性。

Abstract: We introduce the \textbf{Public Discourse Corpus (PDC)}, the first dataset of public-figure interview speech jointly annotated for affective valence and epistemic modality. The corpus contains 998 videos from 100 speakers across seven professional domains, yielding 186,642 sentences (3.1 million words) after sentence segmentation and filtering. To ensure that all retained videos contain analyzable speech from the intended speaker, we introduce \textbf{Target Speaker Participation (TSP)}---a five-category annotation taxonomy with documented inter-annotator reliability ($κ= 0.616$)---as a key methodological contribution that any corpus construction project can adopt. Target-speaker turns are separated from interviewer and third-party speech through an \textbf{audio-first diarization pipeline} combining local Whisper ASR with pyannote speaker separation, released as an open-source implementation. We release the annotated corpus, the annotation tools, the cross-provider validation sample, and the complete processing pipeline. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ictchenbo/public-discourse-corpus.

</details>


### [151] [Lens: Bringing the Right Semantic Perspective into Focus for Training-Free Multimodal Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20252)
*Xinran Liu,Shouqian Shi,Yixian Chen,Ruizhi Chen,Xin-Wei Yao,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Lens 是一种无需训练的框架，通过语义视角锚定和上下文化短语读出，使表示读出任务导向，有效解决语义视角错位问题，显著提升跨任务表示质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义提取方法无法可靠地将提取的状态对齐到下游任务所需的语义视角，导致表示仍受输入显著内容主导，存在语义视角错位问题。

Method: 提出 Lens 框架，通过语义视角锚定将任务所需视角与特定读出短语关联，并在完整输入后放置相同短语以聚合其标记状态，实现全上下文访问与锚定视角结合。

Result: 在 36 个 MMEB 数据集上，Lens 实现整体 Precision@1 为 63.9，相比最接近的同骨干训练自由嵌入基线提升 10.2 点，且无需参数更新、架构修改或重排序。

Conclusion: Lens 通过任务导向的表示读出机制，实现了无需训练的高质量表示学习，克服了语义视角错位问题，显著提升了下游任务表现。

Abstract: High-quality representations are essential for a wide range of downstream tasks. Dedicated embedding models are explicitly optimized for representation learning, yet their training data are often more limited in scale and diversity than the massive corpora used to pretrain modern large language models and multimodal large language models. Large-scale pretraining and instruction following enable autoregressive models to select relevant evidence, integrate multimodal information, and infer semantics under different task perspectives, creating a distinctive opportunity for training-free representation learning. However, our analysis reveals that existing semantic-elicitation methods do not reliably orient the extracted states toward the semantic perspective required by the downstream task. Consequently, the resulting representations often remain dominated by salient input content. We characterize this problem as semantic perspective misalignment and propose Lens, a training-free framework that makes representation readout task-directed. Semantic Perspective Anchoring associates the task-required perspective with a task-specific readout phrase, specifying the interpretive role of the positions later used for extraction. Contextualized Phrase Readout places the same phrase after the complete input and aggregates its token states, combining full-context access with the anchored perspective. The resulting representation reflects task-conditioned evidence integration and inference rather than a generic summary of salient content. Without parameter updates, architectural modification, or reranking, Lens achieves an overall Precision@1 of 63.9 across all 36 MMEB datasets, outperforming the closest same-backbone training-free embedding baseline by 10.2 points.

</details>


### [152] [Viveka-Insight: a cross-lingual concept graph and citation-grounded retrieval resource over the complete works of Swami Vivekananda in English and Bengali](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20303)
*Tamal Maharaj*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Viveka-Insight，一个针对印度哲学家辨喜（Swami Vivekananda）作品的双语语言资源与开源处理管道，涵盖约1500万字符的英文《全集》和孟加拉语《瓦尼与著作》。该资源包含四个层次：（i）保持结构的解析（32,694段落，168,842句），带有指向出版版本的深度链接；（ii）包含8,362个跨语言概念的语义图谱，通过英语标签作为字符串等价键实现无平行数据下的跨语言对齐；（iii）包含60,850个表面形式的双语别名库；（iv）由三位标注者标注的200个段落-概念关系的人工标注集。评估显示跨语言检索召回率在10位内达0.86，引文完整性审计表现良好，概念提取在严格共识下精度为0.60（Cohen's kappa=0.61），置信度≥0.8时精度提升至0.71且保留98%的概念段落。孟加拉语端精度较低（0.54 vs 0.68），凸显跨语言访问的瓶颈所在。整体设计可推广至其他多语言古典文献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古典哲学文献面临三大挑战：多语言且无平行对齐、词汇与现代读者脱节、生成内容需在文化敏感材料上具备可验证依据。为解决这些问题，需要构建可跨语言访问、语义清晰且可追溯的资源。

Method: 构建基于结构保留解析、跨语言概念图谱、双语别名库与人工标注的多层资源体系；利用英语作为语义锚点实现无平行数据下的跨语言对齐；通过人工标注与自动提取结合进行质量评估与校准。

Result: 跨语言检索在194对已验证演讲中达到0.86的召回率（双向）；引文完整性和现代问题连接性审计通过；人类评估显示概念提取在严格共识下精度为0.60（Kappa=0.61）；置信度≥0.8时精度升至0.71，覆盖98%概念段落；孟加拉语端精度显著低于英文（0.54 vs 0.68），暴露系统弱点。

Conclusion: Viveka-Insight为古典多语言文本提供了一种可复用的资源构建范式，尤其适用于缺乏平行数据的哲学与宗教文献。其核心优势在于通过语义锚点实现跨语言对齐，同时保证可追溯性与可验证性。尽管孟加拉语端仍有改进空间，但整体框架具备良好的扩展潜力。

Abstract: Classical philosophical corpora pose three compounding challenges for language resources: they exist in several languages without parallel alignment, their vocabulary is remote from that of contemporary readers, and generated text over culturally sensitive material must be verifiably grounded. We present Viveka-Insight, a bilingual resource and open-source pipeline for the works of Swami Vivekananda (1863-1902): the nine-volume English Complete Works and the ten-volume Bengali Vani o Rachana, two related but non-parallel corpora of about 15 million characters. Four layers are released: (i) a structure-preserving parse (32,694 paragraphs, 168,842 sentences) with per-paragraph anchors deep-linking into the published editions; (ii) a cross-lingual concept graph of 8,362 language-agnostic concepts with 87,518 relation-typed paragraph-concept and 55,872 concept-concept edges, in which canonical English labels act as a string-equality key linking Bengali and English passages with no parallel data; (iii) a bilingual alias inventory of 60,850 surface forms (30,053 English, 30,797 Bengali); and (iv) a human-annotated set of 200 paragraph-concept edges judged by three annotators, released with all per-annotator judgments. We report known-item cross-lingual retrieval over 194 verified rendered lecture pairs (Recall@10 0.86 in both directions), a 30-question audit of citation integrity and modern-question bridging, and a human study placing concept-extraction precision at 0.60 under strict two-annotator consensus (Cohen's kappa = 0.61). The extractor's confidence weight is calibrated: restricting to weight >= 0.8 raises precision to 0.71 while retaining 98% of concept-bearing paragraphs. Precision is markedly lower in Bengali than English (0.54 vs 0.68), locating the weakness in exactly the half that cross-lingual access depends on. The design transfers to other multilingual classical corpora.

</details>


### [153] [Schema-Anchored Latent Reasoning for Semantic Parsing-Based Knowledge Base Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20398)
*Guangze Gao,Zixuan Li,Sikui Zhang,Chunfeng Yuan,Wenjuan Li,Bing Li,Xiaolong Jin,Weiming Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SALR提出一种基于模式锚定的潜在推理方法，通过在模型隐藏状态中生成连续思维来延迟对逻辑形式（LF）决策的显式承诺，从而避免早期错误决策的传播。它利用从真实LF中确定性推导出的模式轨迹，通过一个对齐目标将连续思维与知识库模式元素的代码本对齐，并将对齐后的模式代码引入后续推理步骤，实现模式引导的反馈。该方法无需模型输出显式的文本推理路径，显著提升了复杂问题上的表现，在GrailQA和WebQSP上均优于现有基线，尤其在组合型问题上比TIARA高出2.86 F1点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在处理大型异构知识库时，面临如何选择相关模式元素并构建复杂逻辑形式的挑战。现有方法常在中间推理阶段过早做出离散的模式选择，导致错误决策传播，影响最终结果。

Method: 提出SALR方法，采用多步推理，生成模型隐藏状态中的连续思维以延迟显式决策；通过代码本对齐目标将连续思维与知识库模式元素对齐，并将对齐的模式代码作为输入传递至后续步骤，实现模式引导的反馈机制。

Result: 在GrailQA和WebQSP数据集上，SALR consistently超越强基线；在GrailQA的组合问题上，比TIARA提升2.86 F1点；分析表明，模式引导反馈有效影响了逻辑形式生成，且模式信息可从隐状态中恢复。

Conclusion: SALR通过模式锚定的潜在推理机制，有效缓解了早期错误决策问题，提升了复杂逻辑形式生成的准确性，为基于大模型的语义解析提供了新思路。

Abstract: Semantic parsing (SP)-based knowledge base question answering aims to answer natural language questions by generating executable logical forms (LFs) over knowledge bases (KBs). When applying Large Language Models (LLMs) to this task, a key challenge over large, heterogeneous KBs is selecting question-related schema elements (i.e., relations and classes) and composing them into complex LFs. Recent LLM-based methods often make early discrete commitments to schema elements during intermediate reasoning, allowing incorrect intermediate schema decisions to propagate and finally result in incorrect LFs. To overcome this limitation, we propose SALR, a schema-anchored latent reasoning method for LF construction. It performs multi-step reasoning by generating continuous thoughts in the model's hidden states, thereby delaying the explicit commitment to LF decisions. To ground this latent reasoning process in the corresponding KB schema, SALR aligns continuous thoughts with a codebook of KB schema elements through an alignment objective supervised by schema traces deterministically derived from gold LFs. It then incorporates the aligned schema codes into inputs for subsequent reasoning steps. This schema-mediated feedback guides LF generation without requiring the model to emit an explicit textual reasoning trajectory. Experiments on GrailQA and WebQSP show that SALR achieves consistent overall gains over strong baselines. Notably, on compositional questions from GrailQA, SALR outperforms TIARA, a strong SP-based baseline, by 2.86 F1 points. Further analyses show that schema-mediated feedback affects LF generation and that schema information is recoverable from the latent states.

</details>


### [154] [Xeno-Interpretability: Investigating the Alien Minds of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20408)
*F. Pierucci,M. Bracale Syrnikov,M. Prandi,M. Galisai,F. Giarrusso,P. Bisconti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文探讨大语言模型中可能存在人类无法理解的内部表征（称为异化表征，xeno-representations），并提出‘异化可解释性’的概念。研究指出，模型内部可能存在的区分远超人类语言所能描述的范围，即使这些表征无法用人类概念表达，仍可通过实验定位、几何刻画和因果干预来研究其行为影响。论文建议建立一种实证方法识别此类表征，并强调其在人工智能安全与多智能体系统中的潜在风险，主张可解释性的目标应从寻找人类概念转向理解模型自身的原生表征结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型的解释依赖于人类已有的概念（如真实性、有害性等），但这些概念无法覆盖模型内部所有可能的区分。作者质疑：是否存在人类无法命名或理解的内部表征？这促使提出‘异化可解释性’以探索模型原生的、非人类中心的表征空间。

Method: 通过理论分析与概念建模，区分人类可解释的语义空间与模型原生的异化语义空间；提出实验识别与因果操控的方法，即便无法进行语义解释，也可验证内部表示的功能作用；构建一个以实证为基础的探索框架，用于发现和刻画异化表征。

Result: 证明了模型内部可能存在的区分数量远超人类语言描述能力；展示了即使缺乏人类语义解释，仍可对内部表示进行定位、表征和因果干预；提出了可行的实证路径来识别异化表征，并揭示其在多智能体系统和AI安全中的潜在影响。

Conclusion: 异化可解释性将可解释性的目标从寻找人类概念转向发现和理解模型自身原生的、可能影响行为但不可预测的表征结构，这对提升模型安全性与可控性具有重要意义。

Abstract: Large language models are usually interpreted through concepts that humans already possess: truthfulness, refusal, deception, personality, harmfulness, and related categories. This paper asks whether models may also represent and use distinctions for which no adequate human concept exists. We call such internal structures xeno-representations, and their study xeno-interpretability. We distinguish the human-interpretable semantic space from the xeno-semantic space: the region of model-native representations for which no adequate human conceptual counterpart is available. We show that the space of possible internal distinctions in an LLM is substantially larger than the space available through finite human descriptions. We then separate experimental identification from semantic interpretation: an internal representation may be reproducibly located, geometrically characterized, causally manipulated, and linked to downstream behaviour even when its semantic content cannot be adequately expressed in human terms. On this basis, we sketch an empirical programme to identify xeno-representations. We finally examine the implications for AI safety and multi-agent systems, where model-native representations may propagate and stabilize across interacting agents while remaining only partially visible through human-readable communication. Xeno-interpretability therefore shifts the aim of interpretability from finding human concepts inside models toward discovering and characterizing the representational structures that are native to the models themselves and might affect their behaviour in unpredictable ways.

</details>


### [155] [Stress-testing Alignment Midtraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20412)
*Sid Baines,Jonathan Bostock,Maria Angelica Martinez,Andrew Draganov,David Africa,Daniel Tan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究评估了中训练（AMT）在对前沿模型进行对齐时的有效性，发现尽管AMT在某些简单场景下能引导模型动机，但当存在少量与目标动机冲突的微调数据时，其效果会被完全抵消。此外，若规则仅在中训练或微调阶段的部分数据中出现，则模型难以稳健学习这些规则。总体而言，目前缺乏充分的公开证据支持中训练能有效解决强大AI系统对齐的核心难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对齐前沿模型主要依赖后训练技术，但无法覆盖所有部署场景，因此需要模型具备泛化能力。中训练（AMT）作为一种可能提升泛化性的方法被提出，但其实际有效性尚未得到充分验证。

Method: 通过大规模实验评估中训练的有效性，涵盖高达1100亿参数的模型和10亿个中训练标记。研究包括：1）在后训练数据存在模糊动机的情况下测试中训练的引导能力；2）考察不同规则演示在中训练与后训练数据中的分布对模型学习的影响；3）系统分析多种假设在不同规模下的表现。

Result: 1）在仅有轻微竞争动机的数据干扰下，中训练的效果被完全消除；2）规则必须在中训练或后训练数据中明确展示，才能被模型稳健学习；3）中训练在复杂、现实情境中未能展现出可靠且可推广的对齐效果。

Conclusion: 目前缺乏足够的公共证据支持中训练能够有效应对强大人工智能系统对齐的核心挑战，其在真实部署环境中的可靠性仍存疑。

Abstract: When aligning frontier models through post-training techniques, it is not possible to directly demonstrate all of the behaviours we want a model to exhibit in all possible deployment environments; our model must generalise outside of the post-training distribution. One proposed solution is alignment midtraining (AMT), which continues pretraining on large volumes of alignment-relevant documents to encourage generalisation in later stages of training.
  Despite the prominence of AMT as an alignment approach, there is limited public evidence for its effectiveness. To resolve this, we identify several assumptions around midtraining and evaluate them across scale: up to 110 billion-parameter models and 1 billion midtraining tokens. For instance, we study a scenario where post-training data is ambiguous between two possible motivations. We find that midtraining can steer the model's motivation in simple versions of this setting. However, the presence of a tiny fraction of finetuning data which suggests a competing motivation erases the effects of AMT. We also study scenarios in which we want an AI to follow a number of rules, but only demonstrate a subset of them. We find that demonstrations must be present either in midtraining or post-training datasets for these rules to be robustly learned.
  Based on these and other findings, we do not believe that there is sufficient public evidence for us to confidently state that midtraining can address the core difficulties inherent in aligning powerful AI systems.

</details>


### [156] [Edustories: A Collection of Real-world Case Studies from Classroom Practices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20484)
*Michal Štefánik,Jan Nehyba,Jirina Karasova,Martin Fico,Lucie Škarková,Markéta Košatková,David Kosatka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Edustories数据集，包含1,492个教师撰写的案例研究，描述了中小学课堂中具有挑战性的学生行为、教学干预及其结果。该数据集可用于评估大语言模型（LLM）预测教学干预成功的能力。实验表明，尽管当前最强的模型达到58%准确率，但仍低于人类专家的64%准确率，显示出AI在集体教学辅助中的潜力与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI教育研究多关注个性化学生支持，但实际教学仍以集体课堂为主。亟需研究AI在集体教学场景中的应用，因此需要真实课堂情境的数据集来评估AI辅助能力。

Method: 构建Edustories数据集，包含教师撰写的1,492个真实课堂案例；利用四个语言模型家族的最新模型，评估其预测教学干预结果的能力，并与人类专家评估结果对比。

Result: 当前最强的LLM模型在预测教学干预成功方面达到58%准确率，而人类专家为64%，表明模型仍不及人类，但具备一定潜力。

Conclusion: Edustories数据集为研究AI在集体教学中的作用提供了重要基础；尽管现有模型表现尚不如人类专家，但已展现出作为教师辅助工具的初步潜力，未来仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Despite the widely recognized potential of AI in education, most prior work has focused on individualized student assistance. In contrast, the majority of educational practice worldwide still takes place in collective classroom settings. To enable researchers to study AI assistance in collective teaching, we introduce Edustories, a dataset of 1,492 teacher-written case studies describing real elementary and high-school classroom situations involving challenging student behavior, pedagogical interventions, and their outcomes. Among many other applications, Edustories enables evaluating LLMs' ability to predict the success of teacher interventions, crucial for providing practicing teachers with useful feedback. Comparing the latest models from four language-model families against expert assessments, we find that current models fall short of human expertise in predicting classroom outcomes; the strongest models reach 58% accuracy compared to 64% of human experts. This gap highlights both the limitations and the emerging potential of AI as assistants for practicing teachers.

</details>


### [157] [Relational Attention for Data-Efficient Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20530)
*Adrian Brasoveanu,Ece Takmaz,Jakub Dotlačil*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Relational BabyLM introduces a Dual Attention Transformer (DAT) that separates object-level and relational information in a decoder-only Transformer, improving data efficiency and generalization. It uses Next-Latent Prediction (NextLat) to compress history into dense belief states and a novel RoPE-based symbol-retrieval mechanism with no extra parameters. On the 100M-word track, the model ranks 6th overall and 3rd on NLP tasks, outperforming GPT-2 on most benchmarks, with the highest EWoK score among strict-track entries.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To improve data efficiency and out-of-training-sample generalization in language modeling by incorporating cognitively inspired inductive biases, particularly disentangling relational from sensory information, while maintaining integration of both for effective language generation.

Method: The system employs a Dual Attention Transformer (DAT) replacing standard self-attention with relational attention (RA), which decouples object-level and structural/relational features. It integrates a Next-Latent Prediction (NextLat) objective for incremental belief state compression and a RoPE-based symbol-retrieval mechanism that avoids parameter increase. Experiments are conducted across different training scales (10M and 100M words) with varying RA configurations.

Result: On the 100M-word track, the best model achieves 6th place overall and 3rd on the NLP subset among 55 entries. It surpasses GPT-2 on most benchmarks, with one model achieving the highest EWoK score among strict-track submissions. Full RA performs better at 100M words, while RA variants are interchangeable at 10M words.

Conclusion: Architecture is the primary driver of structural linguistic generalization, with training objectives playing a secondary but still significant role. The DAT design with relational attention and symbolic retrieval enhances performance under data constraints, demonstrating strong potential for efficient and generalizable language modeling.

Abstract: We present Relational BabyLM, a system submission to the BabyLM 2026 challenge that combines two cognitively motivated inductive biases in a single decoder-only Transformer. Architecturally, we replace standard self-attention with a Dual Attention Transformer (DAT), which separates the routing of object-level ("sensory") lexical features from structural/relational information (Altabaa and Lafferty, 2025; Altabaa et al., 2024; Webb et al., 2024; Kerg et al., 2022; Webb et al., 2021). Relational attention (RA) disentangled from self-attention greatly increases data efficiency and out-of-training-sample generalization on purely relational tasks, but language modeling requires object-level and relational information to be integrated as well as disentangled, and RA-based LMs have remained largely unexplored. BabyLM's data-constrained training and comprehensive evaluation is an ideal testing ground for whether that data efficiency transfers. As a training intervention, we add a Next-Latent Prediction (NextLat; Teoh et al. 2026) objective that encourages hidden states to compress history incrementally into a dense belief state. Architecture is the dominant factor for structural linguistic generalization; the objective is secondary but still significant. DAT's three relational attention types (full RA vs. the simpler RCA and DisRCA variants) are largely interchangeable at 10M words; full RA pulls ahead at 100M. We also introduce a novel symbol-retrieval mechanism (RoPE-based, as opposed to learned, relative symbols) that matches learned symbol libraries while adding no parameters. On the strict (100M-word) track, our best model ranks 6th of 55 overall and 3rd of 55 on the leaderboard's NLP-task subset at the time of writing; our two strongest models outperform the GPT-2 baseline on most benchmarks, with one attaining the highest EWoK score among strict-track entries.

</details>


### [158] [An Analysis of Training-Free Self-Reported Confidence in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20541)
*Lukas Meyer,Sofia Rossi,Wei Chen,Thomas Laurent,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了大语言模型在生成内容时附带的数值置信度是否具有实际意义，发现直接口头表达置信度是强基线（正确性预测的AUROC达0.956和0.937），而三样本一致性表现较弱，且置信度易受提示方式影响，存在共性错误放大问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型自报告置信度的真实有效性，判断其是否超越表面化表述，而非仅反映训练中的校准修辞。

Method: 分析三种无需训练的信号：答案中口头表达的置信度、后验概率 $P(\mathrm{True})$、同一问题三次生成结果的一致性；在100个TriviaQA问题上对两个模型家族进行评估，并通过重新提示测试置信度的稳定性。

Result: 口头表达置信度表现优异（AUROC 0.956/0.937），三样本一致性较差（0.765/0.790），置信度随提示变化显著（平均变动0.043–0.084），部分决策在0.8阈值下翻转4%–9%；且支持与反驳主张间的置信度差距较小，表明模型自报告仍受提示方式、相关错误和基准噪声影响。

Conclusion: 模型的自报告置信度虽具一定预测能力，但其可靠性高度依赖于提示方式、样本一致性及基准数据质量，无法完全信任为客观指标，需谨慎使用。

Abstract: Large language models can report a numerical confidence together with generated content, but it is unclear whether this report is more than calibrated rhetoric. We analyze three training-free signals: confidence verbalized with the answer, post-hoc $P(\mathrm{True})$, and agreement with three additional generations on the same 100 TriviaQA questions for two model families. Direct verbalization is a surprisingly strong baseline: after auditing benchmark errors, it reaches AUROC 0.956 and 0.937 for correctness prediction. Three-sample agreement is substantially weaker (0.765 and 0.790), and a fixed interpolation with verbalized confidence has no statistically reliable benefit. Four of nine errors from one model and two of eight from the other receive unanimous sample support, showing that self-consistency can amplify shared misconceptions. Re-eliciting confidence for the same fixed answers with equivalent prompts changes scores by 0.043 to 0.084 on average and flips 4\% to 9\% of decisions at a 0.8 threshold. An exploratory audit of 100 confidence-tagged biography claims further finds only a modest confidence gap between supported and contradicted claims. These results argue that useful self-reports remain sensitive to elicitation, correlated errors, and benchmark noise.

</details>


### [159] [Steering the Compass: Aligning Dynamic Psychological Counseling Conversations with Cognitive Behavioral Therapy Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20565)
*Zimu Wang,Yiwen Jiang,Xiangyu Zhao,Yaling Shen,Jiahe Liu,Stephanie Fong,Maxmartwell H Cheng,Guilherme C Oliveira,Anh Nguyen,Robert Desimone,Barnaby Nelson,Dominic Dwyer,Zongyuan Ge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出StratCBT数据集，包含9,688个心理咨询会话和约25.6万条对话，每条咨询师回复均对应八种不同认知行为疗法（CBT）策略。通过基于负面思维建模客户并使用自对话生成高质量会话，该数据集在通用咨询和CBT特定技能方面均超越现有数据集。实验验证了策略对齐生成的有效性，并在大语言模型模拟客户的情境下评估其专业性和疗效，推动了动态决策在心理辅导中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前心理辅导研究中，尽管认知行为疗法（CBT）依赖于根据客户实时心理状态进行动态决策，但这一关键因素常被忽视，限制了系统的灵活性与治疗效果。因此亟需更精细、策略明确的数据支持以提升模型的适应性与专业性。

Method: 采用自对话（self-chat）方法，基于客户负面思维构建心理状态模型，生成真实感强的心理咨询会话；通过引入实际会话作为指导，确保生成内容的质量与策略一致性，最终构建包含8类CBT策略标注的大型数据集StratCBT。

Result: StratCBT在通用心理咨询及CBT特定技能方面表现优于现有数据集；实验表明，策略对齐生成显著提升了大语言模型在模拟真实情境下的咨询质量与专业性，增强了动态决策能力。

Conclusion: StratCBT为心理辅导领域提供了首个大规模、策略明确的对话数据集，有效推动了基于大语言模型的心理咨询系统在动态决策与个性化干预方面的进步，具有重要的研究与应用价值。

Abstract: Recent advancements in large language models have revolutionized the field of psychological counseling, especially in the context of Cognitive Behavioral Therapy (CBT). While the success of CBT relies heavily on dynamic decision-making informed by the client's real-time mental state, this aspect has often been overlooked in current research, limiting both flexibility and therapeutic outcomes. In this paper, we introduce StratCBT, a dataset specifically designed for psychological counseling conversations with CBT Strategies, consisting of 9,688 sessions and around 256K utterances, with each counselor's response aligned with one of eight distinct strategies. The creation of StratCBT involves modeling clients based on their negative thoughts and generating high-quality counseling conversations through self-chat, incorporating realistic sessions as guidance, thereby significantly surpassing existing datasets in both general counseling and CBT-specific skills. We conduct extensive experiments to demonstrate the effectiveness of strategy-aligned generation and evaluate its efficacy in delivering professional and effective counseling with LLM-simulated clients to reflect real-world scenarios. The dataset can be obtained from https://github.com/zimuwangnlp/StratCBT.

</details>


### [160] [SAFARI: An Industrial Benchmark for LLM-Assisted Hazard Analysis and Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20584)
*Chenxi Wu,Zimu Wang,Haiyang Zhang,Wei Wang,Zhijie Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SAFARI是首个面向汽车HARA任务的工业级基准，包含3000个去标识化案例，评估LLM在开放性危害分析和标准驱动风险评估中的表现。研究提出首个参考锚定的LLM作为裁判协议，验证了专家一致性。实验显示，尽管模型能生成合理危害描述，但在ISO 26262风险分类上表现不佳（最佳ASIL宏平均F1仅为0.261），且思维链提示效果有限甚至有害。主要错误源于危害生成中关键场景信息遗漏及风险评估中可控性误判，提示应重点加强专家监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大语言模型在安全关键工程中的可靠性，尤其是在符合ISO 26262标准的功能安全流程中的应用潜力，填补当前对LLM在汽车HARA任务中表现评估的空白。

Method: 构建SAFARI基准数据集，包含3000个真实工业去标识化HARA案例；设计参考锚定的LLM-as-a-judge评估协议以衡量开放性危害分析质量；采用九个前沿大语言模型进行实验，对比不同提示策略（如Chain-of-Thought）在风险分类任务中的表现，并进行误差分析定位系统性缺陷。

Result: LLM在生成合理危害叙述方面表现尚可，但在依据ISO 26262进行风险分类时能力有限，最佳模型的ASIL宏平均F1仅为0.261；思维链提示对分类任务帮助甚微，甚至降低性能；主要失败模式集中在危害生成阶段遗漏关键场景上下文，以及风险评估阶段对可控性的错误判断。

Conclusion: 尽管大语言模型在辅助汽车功能安全中的危害分析方面展现出一定潜力，但其在标准合规性风险评估方面仍存在显著不足，需依赖专家干预来确保安全性与合规性，未来工作应聚焦于提升模型对场景上下文和可控性判断的理解能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly considered for safety-critical engineering, yet their reliability in regulated functional-safety workflows remains underexplored. We introduce SAFARI (Safety-Aware Functional Automotive Risk Inference), the first industrial benchmark for LLM-assisted automotive Hazard Analysis and Risk Assessment (HARA) under ISO 26262. It contains 3,000 de-identified industrial HARA cases and evaluates two coupled tasks: open-ended hazard analysis and standards-grounded risk assessment. To evaluate open-ended HARA artifacts, we propose the first reference-anchored LLM-as-a-judge protocol with high expert correlation. Experiments with nine frontier LLMs show that models often produce plausible hazard narratives but remain weak at ISO 26262 risk classification, with the best ASIL macro-F1 reaching only 0.261. Chain-of-Thought prompting provides limited benefit and often degrades categorical risk assessment. Error analysis further localizes major failures to scenario-critical context omissions during hazard generation and to controllability misjudgments during risk assessment, indicating where expert oversight should be concentrated. The dataset can be obtained from https://github.com/xixi47520-hash/HARA.

</details>


### [161] [WiC is Not WSD: A Study on LLMs and Lexical Ambiguity Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20593)
*Yi Zhou,Kiamehr Rezaee,Danushka Bollegala,Mohammad Taher Pilehvar,Jose Camacho-Collados*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了词汇在上下文（WiC）任务中对语言模型的挑战，发现其困难不仅源于比较同一词在不同上下文中的用法，还因缺乏明确的语义粒度层级。通过对比开放语言模型在WiC与传统词义消歧（WSD）任务中的表现，研究显示提供候选词义显著提升WiC性能，说明显式语义信息有助于模型做出更一致、精准的判断。人类评估进一步表明，许多看似错误的判断实为标签模糊或模型与标注者对意义边界理解不一致所致，且大模型常因过度细化语义区分而产生错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WiC任务对语言模型仍具挑战性，现有方法未能充分解决语义粒度不明确的问题，导致模型难以准确判断词语在上下文中的确切含义。

Method: 在相似设置下评估开放语言模型在WiC和传统WSD任务上的表现，引入候选词义作为外部知识，分析其对模型性能的影响；并通过人工评估验证模型错误的成因。

Result: 提供候选词义显著提升了语言模型在WiC任务上的表现；人类评估揭示多数错误源于标签模糊或语义边界差异，而非单纯的词汇理解失败；大模型倾向于过度细分语义，从而引发错误。

Conclusion: 显式提供词义候选能有效改善语言模型在WiC任务中的表现，且当前模型错误多由语义边界不一致或过度精细化引起，建议在设计任务时考虑语义粒度的一致性与标注标准的清晰性。

Abstract: Word-in-Context (WiC) remains challenging for language models, despite recent progress on lexical-semantic tasks. We hypothesise that this difficulty arises not only from comparing two contextual uses of a word, but also from the absence of an explicit sense inventory that specifies the relevant level of semantic granularity. We evaluate open LLMs on WiC and traditional Word Sense Disambiguation (WSD) under similar settings. We find that providing candidate senses, similar to what is done in traditional WSD, improves WiC performance in all settings. In general, explicit sense information helps models make more consistent and targeted judgements. Human evaluation further shows that many apparent WiC errors reflect label ambiguity or mismatches between model and annotator sense boundaries rather than simple failures of lexical understanding. In particular, results show that LLMs overthink the sense distinction often leading to errors based on overly fine-grained distinctions.

</details>


### [162] [What Does Privileged Information Add to On-Policy Self-Distillation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20612)
*XiuYu Zhang,Wei Chow,Junfeng Fang,Zhenkai Liang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过构建AMPLE-Math数据集，系统评估了在策略自蒸馏（OPSD）中使用教师模型提供额外解题参考信息的价值。结果表明，参考信息的增益主要体现在促进直接响应与思维推理模式之间的跨模式迁移，而非单纯揭示更多解题细节。即使在学生模型未充分训练时，参考信息也带来一定提升，但其作用依赖于学生训练状态。此外，改变教师的逐标记监督对学生的实际行为影响有限，说明蒸馏的核心价值在于参数共享带来的能力访问，而非参考内容本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在策略自蒸馏（OPSD）中，教师模型提供的额外解题参考信息是否真正带来了显著收益，还是仅是蒸馏过程本身的体现。目标是分离出参考信息的独立贡献，并理解其在不同模型和训练阶段的作用机制。

Method: 构建了一个包含5,319道数学题、六种不同推理视角（共享同一答案）的可复用数据集AMPLE-Math；在Qwen3-1.7B和SmolLM3-3B模型上进行对比实验，比较不同推理视图下的参考自由蒸馏效果；采用匹配的损失干预与教师行为分析，验证监督方式对学生产生的影响。

Result: 参考自由蒸馏已解释了Qwen3-1.7B在思维启用评估下的大部分性能提升；参考信息的附加收益较弱，仅在特定情况下（如优化后的解题路径）有明显表现；在相同检查点下，将短直接响应改为长思维推理反而导致性能下降；教师的逐标记监督变化并未显著改变学生行为。

Conclusion: OPSD的价值主要体现在通过共享参数实现直接响应与思维推理模式间的跨模式能力迁移，而参考信息的真正价值在于其对这种迁移的促进作用，而非它所揭示的具体解题细节。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) lets a language model learn from a frozen copy of itself that sees an answer or a worked solution. Giving the teacher this extra information seems to offer the student more to learn, but how much does it add beyond distillation itself? To isolate that contribution, we construct AMPLE-Math, a reusable suite of 5,319 mathematical problems with six reasoning views that share the same answer, and compare each view with matched reference-free distillation. With a thinking-enabled teacher supervising direct-response rollouts, reference-free distillation accounts for much of Qwen3-1.7B's improvement under thinking-enabled evaluation, both in domain and on external benchmarks. Evidence for an additional reference benefit is modest in Qwen, strongest for a polished solution, whereas complete traces add two percentage points in SmolLM3-3B at step 50. These benefits depend on the student being trained. At the same checkpoint, replacing short direct-response rollouts with long thinking-enabled rollouts turns gains into losses in both families while the problems, references, and evaluation stay fixed. Teacher profiles and matched loss interventions in Qwen further show that changing token-level supervision can leave student behavior largely unchanged. Together, these findings suggest that OPSD can improve access to existing reasoning capabilities through parameters shared by direct-response and thinking-enabled inference. The value of a privileged reference is what it adds to this cross-mode transfer, not how much of the solution it reveals.

</details>


### [163] [Chronicle: Cut-Point Replay for Regression Testing of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20625)
*Tisha Chawla,Susheem Koul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Chronicle记录大语言模型代理运行中的非确定性边界为不可变的封装，并通过切点重放技术，选择性地重放部分边界并实时执行其余部分，实现对代码变更的回归测试。该方法在保持比特稳定性的前提下，将故障重现转化为持续集成中的可测用例，显著提升调试效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型代理的非确定性导致故障难以复现，现有工具仅支持记录和评分，无法用于测试代码变更。需要一种能将运行记录转化为可重复、可验证的回归测试机制。

Method: Chronicle通过在非确定性边界处创建不可变记录包（封装），利用切点重放技术，选择性地从记录中重放部分边界，同时实时执行剩余部分，从而在持续集成中对代码修改进行快速验证。

Result: 在6个已记录的故障案例上，记录开销仅为每跨边界23微秒（占300毫秒模型调用的0.008%），完整重放不产生任何模型调用且20次重复结果一致；切点测试能有效检测错误代码，通过安全与无害变更；在变异测试中，切点测试捕获所有绕过安全检查的变异，而基线方法则全部漏检。

Conclusion: Chronicle成功将非确定性代理运行转化为可重复、可验证的回归测试，具备低开销、高稳定性与强检测能力，适用于持续集成环境下的可靠性保障。

Abstract: Large language model responses are non-deterministic, so failures in LLM agents are hard to reproduce: a failure depends on inference that is not bitwise reproducible, on tools that read changing state, and on a multi-step trajectory that a re-run rarely repeats. Record-and-replay makes a run reproducible, but existing agent tooling records runs only to trace or score them, not to test a code change against them. We present Chronicle, which records an agent run at its non-deterministic boundaries as immutable envelopes and replays it from the record. Its central operation, cut-point replay, serves a chosen subset of boundaries from the record and executes the complementary subset live with new code, turning a recorded incident into a regression test that runs in continuous integration. On a benchmark of 6 recorded failures with simulated model boundaries, recording adds 23 μs per crossing (0.008% of an assumed 300 ms model call), full replay issues zero model calls and is bit-stable across 20 repetitions, and cut-point tests fail on faulty code and pass on guarded and benign changes for all 6 incidents. In a mutation study of the guarded tools, cut-point tests catch every mutant that lets the recorded unsafe action through, while a baseline that stubs every boundary, using the same assertion, catches none. Chronicle and the benchmark are publicly available at https://github.com/theagentplane/chronicle.

</details>


### [164] [UniPolicy: Unified Objective-Specific Policies for Generative Search Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20630)
*Kun Yao,Yuhang Zhou,Yichi Zhang,Zeliang Tong,Shengri Xue,Haitao Wang,Siyu Lu,Qianlong Xie,Xingxing Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniPolicy提出一种目标感知的多策略对齐框架，通过特定目标前缀标记、稀疏MoE-LoRA路由和目标特异性残差前馈网络，在共享骨干网络中分层解耦参数，为不同商业目标提供差异化的参数与策略表达空间。同时利用多阶段行为反馈构建成对偏好，增强点击候选在生成分布中的相对优势。推理时支持并行、业务可定制的多策略束搜索，灵活分配候选名额。离线实验表明其在多个指标上实现平衡提升，线上A/B测试显示CTR提升0.71%，RPS提升1.58%，广告收入提升1.32%，且保持稳定延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索广告系统通常仅对单一商业目标（如eCPM）进行对齐，或采用简单的奖励融合方法，难以兼顾相关性、点击率和商业价值等异构目标，易受梯度竞争影响导致全局性能下降。需要一种能有效协同多目标、避免冲突的统一优化框架。

Method: UniPolicy结合目标特异性前缀标记、稀疏MoE-LoRA路由机制与目标特异性残差前馈网络，实现共享模型中参数的层次化解耦；通过多阶段用户行为反馈构建成对偏好信号，增强点击样本的优势表达；推理阶段支持并行多策略束搜索，按业务需求动态分配候选配额。

Result: 大规模离线实验验证了模型在多指标上的均衡提升，未牺牲召回质量；7天线上A/B测试显示CTR提升0.71%，RPS提升1.58%，广告收入增长1.32%，服务延迟稳定，优于单目标强化学习与简单奖励融合基线。

Conclusion: UniPolicy成功实现了多目标搜索广告系统的高效对齐，通过参数解耦与偏好建模，有效缓解梯度竞争，兼顾用户体验与商业收益，在真实系统中展现出显著性能提升与工程可行性。

Abstract: Search advertising connects user intent with commercial content and plays a critical role in platform monetization. Recent systems typically align pretrained generative models with a single business reward, such as eCPM, or use naive reward fusion for preliminary multi-objective alignment. However, an ideal search advertising system must jointly account for heterogeneous objectives, including relevance, click propensity, and commercial value, to balance user experience and business value while mitigating globally suboptimal performance caused by gradient competition. We propose UniPolicy, an objective-aware multi-policy alignment framework. UniPolicy combines objective-specific prefix tokens, sparse MoE-LoRA routing, and objective-specific residual FFNs to hierarchically decouple parameters within a shared backbone, providing differentiated parameter and policy-expression spaces for different business objectives. It further constructs pairwise preferences from multi-stage behavioral feedback, supplementing the relative preference information in exposed-but-unclicked samples and strengthening the relative advantage of clicked candidates in the generation distribution. At inference, UniPolicy supports parallel, business-customizable multi-policy beam search, flexibly allocating candidate quotas across objectives under a fixed retrieval budget. Large-scale offline experiments show that UniPolicy delivers balanced improvements across multiple metrics while preserving retrieval quality, outperforming single-objective reinforcement learning and naive reward-fusion baselines. In a 7-day online A/B test on a real search advertising system, UniPolicy improves CTR by 0.71%, RPS by 1.58%, and advertising revenue by 1.32%, while maintaining stable serving latency.

</details>


### [165] [HerHealthEval: Evaluating Multilingual and Register-Sensitive Understanding of Women's Health Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20684)
*Hassan Saeed Hassan Albattra,Mazen Mohammed Bahgat,Rahatara Ferdousi,Hana Essam Sayed Ahmed Amrya,Mariam Mousa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出HerHealthEval框架，用于评估大语言模型在多语言女性健康沟通中的理解能力。通过六种不同表达形式（包括标准、临床、通俗、间接、情绪化和故意模糊）的病例，测试模型在跨语言、跨语境下的表现。结果显示，传统评估可能掩盖安全相关缺陷；采用语言不变的风险标签可显著降低误判率，强调了在多语言医疗评估中需关注表达变体、不确定性处理及标签一致性的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在医疗沟通中广泛应用，但多数评估仅关注回答质量，忽视用户关切是否被正确理解。尤其在多语言环境下，表达方式差异可能导致模型误判，影响患者安全。因此需要一个能系统测试模型对不同语言和表达风格下女性健康问题理解能力的评估框架。

Method: 提出HerHealthEval框架，包含英语、法语、现代标准阿拉伯语三种语言的标准化临床案例，每例采用六种不同表达形式：标准、临床、通俗、间接/含蓄、情绪化及故意省略关键信息。使用多语言指令模型及其QLoRA微调版本，在关心分类、风险校准、澄清行为、解析合规性与跨形式一致性等方面进行评估，并对比不同训练策略的效果。

Result: 整体准确率和一致性可能掩盖严重的安全问题。在语言不对称风险监督下，多语言适应模型在法语和阿拉伯语中出现0.994的低风险漏诊率；而通过源语言导出的语言不变风险标签进行重新微调后，该数值分别降至0.572和0.558，显著提升安全性。

Conclusion: 构建稳健的多语言医疗评估体系需显式测试表达形式变化、不确定性识别能力，以及微调标签的来源与语言不变性。仅依赖高准确率指标不足以保证实际应用的安全性，必须引入更精细的评估机制以保障跨语言医疗对话系统的可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in healthcare communication, yet most evaluations emphasize response quality while assuming that the user's concern has been interpreted correctly. We introduce HerHealthEval, a controlled evaluation framework for multilingual understanding of women's-health communication. For each clinical case, HerHealthEval provides matched versions in English, French, and Modern Standard Arabic using six communicative forms: canonical, clinical, layperson, indirect or hedged, emotionally concerned, and deliberately under-specified. The first five express the same underlying concern and retain the same clinical information, whereas the under-specified form intentionally omits relevant details to test whether the model recognizes that clarification is needed. We evaluate a multilingual instruction model and QLoRA-adapted variants on concern classification, risk calibration, clarification behavior, parse compliance, and cross-form consistency. Results reveal that aggregate accuracy and consistency can conceal safety-relevant failures. A multilingual adaptation model reaches 0.994 under-triage in French and Arabic under language-asymmetric risk supervision. A controlled re-adaptation using source-derived, language-invariant risk labels reduces under-triage to 0.572 and 0.558, respectively. These findings show that robust multilingual healthcare evaluation requires explicit testing of register variation, uncertainty handling, and the provenance and invariance of adaptation labels.

</details>


### [166] [Summarization Bias: The Directional Collapse of Objective Projection into Told-Mode Labels in Large Language Models --- A Conceptual Framework and Registered Test Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20712)
*Levent Bulut*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并操作化了总结偏差的概念：大型语言模型（LLMs）系统性地将叙事意义表现为抽象的摘要标签，而非可重建的推断结构。该偏差在两种模式下表现：生成模式中模型倾向于直接陈述情感而非通过间接方式呈现；评估模式中模型更偏好明确表达而非隐含内容，从而可能对高质量文学写作产生负面影响。研究未验证该偏差，但定义了其概念框架，并基于已有研究提供了方向性证据，同时预注册了检验方案以决定是否放弃该构念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究显示大型语言模型在处理叙事文本时存在系统性倾向，即偏向于直接陈述情感和信息，而忽视通过间接线索推断内容的能力。这可能导致对文学创作中‘展示’（shown）手法的低估，进而影响模型作为评价者时对叙事质量的判断。因此有必要识别并量化这种潜在偏差，以避免对写作风格造成负面选择压力。

Method: 本文首先定义了‘总结偏差’这一概念，并将其置于巴鲁特教义（Bulut Doctrine）的‘讲述-展示’轴线中进行分析。接着通过回顾一项独立的可靠性研究数据，寻找与总结偏差方向一致的证据。最后，预注册了一个双模式测试方案，包含明确的决策规则，若结果不支持该偏差则将放弃此构念。

Result: 初步分析表明，已有研究数据呈现出与总结偏差预期一致的方向性特征，尤其是在模型评估阶段对‘讲述’模式的偏好高于‘展示’模式。然而，该结果尚不足以证实偏差的存在，需进一步实验验证。

Conclusion: 总结偏差是一个有待验证的理论构念，目前提供的是概念定义与初步方向性证据。未来需要通过严格控制的实验来检验其在生成与评估两种模式下的表现，以确定其是否存在及影响程度。若无法通过预设的检验标准，则应放弃该构念。

Abstract: This paper introduces and operationalizes summarization bias: a proposed systematic tendency of large language models (LLMs) to represent narrative meaning as an abstract summary label rather than as the reconstructable inferential structure that produces it. Within the Bulut Doctrine, narrative effect is theorized along a told-shown axis: in told mode, emotional and informational content is declared explicitly and requires little reader reconstruction; in shown mode, that content is suppressed at the surface and must be reconstructed from physical cues and indirection (Objective Projection).
